## Parameters

Transcribed from `src/muz/base/fp_params.pyg` (module `fp`) in file order — full list.

- `engine` (SYMBOL, default `auto-config`) — select: auto-config, datalog, bmc, spacer (doc string omits qbmc/tab/clp/ddnf, all of which are code-reachable — see Dead/unreachable)
- `rlimit` (UINT, default 0) — deterministic resource limit, 0 = no limit
- `datalog.default_table` (SYMBOL, default `sparse`) — default table impl: sparse, hashtable, bitvector, interval
- `datalog.default_relation` (SYMBOL, default `pentagon`) — default relation impl: external_relation, pentagon
- `datalog.generate_explanations` (BOOL, default false) — produce fact explanations (datalog engine only)
- `datalog.use_map_names` (BOOL, default true) — use map-file names when displaying tuples
- `datalog.magic_sets_for_queries` (BOOL, default false) — apply magic-set transform to queries
- `datalog.explanations_on_relation_level` (BOOL, default false) — explanations per relation vs. per fact
- `datalog.unbound_compressor` (BOOL, default true) — introduce aux relations to avoid unbound head vars
- `datalog.similarity_compressor` (BOOL, default true) — merge rules differing only in constants
- `datalog.similarity_compressor_threshold` (UINT, default 11) — min similar-rule count to trigger merge
- `datalog.all_or_nothing_deltas` (BOOL, default false) — delta relations only track modified/not
- `datalog.compile_with_widening` (BOOL, default false) — use widening for recursive rule compilation
- `datalog.default_table_checked` (BOOL, default false) — wrap default table in a checked wrapper
- `datalog.default_table_checker` (SYMBOL, default `null`) — checker table name for the above
- `datalog.check_relation` (SYMBOL, default `null`) — relation name to verify via SMT solving
- `datalog.initial_restart_timeout` (UINT, default 0) — ms before first saturation restart, 0 = none
- `datalog.timeout` (UINT, default 0) — time limit for saturation
- `datalog.output_profile` (BOOL, default false) — output profile info when printing rules/instructions
- `datalog.print.tuples` (BOOL, default true) — print output-predicate tuples
- `datalog.profile_timeout_milliseconds` (UINT, default 0) — hide fast instructions/rules below this threshold
- `datalog.dbg_fpr_nonempty_relation_signature` (BOOL, default false) — avoid empty-signature inner relation in finite_product_relation
- `datalog.subsumption` (BOOL, default true) — filter predicates with total transitions
- `generate_proof_trace` (BOOL, default false) — trace 'sat' answer as a proof object
- `spacer.push_pob` (BOOL, default false) — push blocked pobs to higher level
- `spacer.push_pob_max_depth` (UINT, default UINT_MAX) — max depth for push_pob
- `validate` (BOOL, default false) — validate result via proof/model checking
- `spacer.simplify_lemmas_pre` (BOOL, default false) — simplify lemmas before inductive propagation
- `spacer.simplify_lemmas_post` (BOOL, default false) — simplify lemmas after inductive propagation
- `spacer.use_inductive_generalizer` (BOOL, default true) — generalize lemmas via induction strengthening
- `spacer.max_num_contexts` (UINT, default 500) — max solver contexts to create
- `print_fixedpoint_extensions` (BOOL, default true) — use SMT-LIB2 fixedpoint extensions when printing
- `print_low_level_smt2` (BOOL, default false) — faster but less readable SMT2 printer
- `print_with_variable_declarations` (BOOL, default true) — use variable decls instead of original names
- `print_answer` (BOOL, default false) — print answer instance(s) to a query
- `print_certificate` (BOOL, default false) — print reachability/non-reachability certificate
- `print_boogie_certificate` (BOOL, default false) — print certificate in Boogie-readable format
- `print_aig` (SYMBOL, default '') — dump clauses in AIG (AAG) text format to a file
- `print_statistics` (BOOL, default false) — print statistics
- `tab.selection` (SYMBOL, default `weight`) — TAB engine selection strategy: weight, first, var-use
- `xform.bit_blast` (BOOL, default false) — bit-blast bit-vectors
- `xform.magic` (BOOL, default false) — symbolic magic-set transformation
- `xform.scale` (BOOL, default false) — add scaling variable to LRA clauses
- `xform.inline_linear` (BOOL, default true) — try linear inlining
- `xform.inline_eager` (BOOL, default true) — try eager rule inlining
- `xform.inline_linear_branch` (BOOL, default false) — linear inlining with potential expansion
- `xform.compress_unbound` (BOOL, default true) — compress tails with unbound variables
- `xform.fix_unbound_vars` (BOOL, default false) — fix unbound variables in tail
- `xform.unfold_rules` (UINT, default 0) — unfold rules via iterative squaring
- `xform.slice` (BOOL, default true) — simplify clause set via slicing
- `spacer.use_euf_gen` (BOOL, default false) — generalize using implied equalities
- `xform.transform_arrays` (BOOL, default false) — rewrite array equalities, select-over-store
- `xform.instantiate_arrays` (BOOL, default false) — turn P(a) into P(i, a[i], a)
- `xform.instantiate_arrays.enforce` (BOOL, default false) — turn P(a) into P(i, a[i]), drop a
- `xform.instantiate_arrays.nb_quantifier` (UINT, default 1) — number of quantifiers per array
- `xform.instantiate_arrays.slice_technique` (SYMBOL, default `no-slicing`) — no-slicing vs smash
- `xform.quantify_arrays` (BOOL, default false) — create quantified Horn clauses from array clauses
- `xform.instantiate_quantifiers` (BOOL, default false) — E-matching instantiation of quantified clauses
- `xform.coalesce_rules` (BOOL, default false) — coalesce rules
- `xform.tail_simplifier_pve` (BOOL, default true) — propagate variable equivalences
- `xform.subsumption_checker` (BOOL, default true) — enable subsumption checker (no model conversion support)
- `xform.coi` (BOOL, default true) — cone-of-influence simplification
- `spacer.order_children` (UINT, default 0) — child-enqueue order for non-linear rules: 0 orig, 1 reverse, 2 random
- `spacer.use_lemma_as_cti` (BOOL, default false) — use a lemma instead of a CTI in flexible_trace
- `spacer.reset_pob_queue` (BOOL, default true) — reset pob queue on entering a new level
- `spacer.use_array_eq_generalizer` (BOOL, default true) — generalize lemmas with array equalities
- `spacer.use_derivations` (BOOL, default true) — cache intermediate results for non-linear rules
- `xform.array_blast` (BOOL, default false) — Ackermannize local array terms (some may remain)
- `xform.array_blast_full` (BOOL, default false) — eliminate all local array vars by QE
- `xform.elim_term_ite` (BOOL, default false) — eliminate term-ite expressions
- `xform.elim_term_ite.inflation` (UINT, default 3) — max inflation for non-Boolean ite blasting
- `spacer.propagate` (BOOL, default true) — enable propagate/pushing phase
- `spacer.max_level` (UINT, default UINT_MAX) — max level to explore
- `spacer.elim_aux` (BOOL, default true) — eliminate auxiliary variables in reachability facts
- `spacer.blast_term_ite_inflation` (UINT, default 3) — max inflation for non-Boolean ite expansion
- `spacer.reach_dnf` (BOOL, default true) — restrict reachability facts to DNF
- `bmc.linear_unrolling_depth` (UINT, default UINT_MAX) — max level to explore (BMC)
- `spacer.iuc.split_farkas_literals` (BOOL, default false) — split Farkas literals
- `spacer.native_mbp` (BOOL, default true) — use Z3's native MBP (vs. shared qe/mbp route)
- `spacer.eq_prop` (BOOL, default true) — equality/bound propagation in arithmetic
- `spacer.weak_abs` (BOOL, default true) — weak abstraction
- `spacer.restarts` (BOOL, default false) — enable resetting obligation queue
- `spacer.restart_initial_threshold` (UINT, default 10) — initial restart threshold
- `spacer.random_seed` (UINT, default 0) — random seed for internal SMT solvers
- `spacer.mbqi` (BOOL, default true) — enable MBQI
- `spacer.keep_proxy` (BOOL, default true) — keep proxy variables (internal)
- `spacer.q3` (BOOL, default true) — allow quantified lemmas in frames
- `spacer.q3.instantiate` (BOOL, default true) — instantiate quantified lemmas
- `spacer.q3.use_qgen` (BOOL, default false) — use quantified lemma generalizer
- `spacer.q3.qgen.normalize` (BOOL, default true) — normalize cube before quantified generalization
- `spacer.iuc` (UINT, default 1) — 0 old unsat-core gen, 1 new IUC gen, 2 new IUC + min-cut
- `spacer.iuc.arith` (UINT, default 1) — 0 simple Farkas, 1 Farkas+old-style constant, 2 Gaussian-elim (broken), 3 additive IUC
- `spacer.iuc.old_hyp_reducer` (BOOL, default false) — old hypothesis reducer (debug only)
- `spacer.validate_lemmas` (BOOL, default false) — validate each lemma after generalization
- `spacer.ground_pobs` (BOOL, default true) — ground pobs using model values
- `spacer.iuc.print_farkas_stats` (BOOL, default false) — print per-proof Farkas-lemma stats (debug)
- `spacer.iuc.debug_proof` (BOOL, default false) — print proof used by unsat-core-learner (debug)
- `spacer.simplify_pob` (BOOL, default false) — simplify pobs by removing redundant constraints
- `spacer.p3.share_lemmas` (BOOL, default false) — share frame lemmas
- `spacer.p3.share_invariants` (BOOL, default false) — share invariant lemmas
- `spacer.min_level` (UINT, default 0) — minimal level to explore
- `spacer.trace_file` (SYMBOL, default '') — log file for progress events
- `spacer.ctp` (BOOL, default true) — enable counterexample-to-pushing
- `spacer.use_inc_clause` (BOOL, default true) — use incremental clause to represent trans
- `spacer.dump_benchmarks` (BOOL, default false) — dump SMT queries as benchmarks
- `spacer.dump_threshold` (DOUBLE, default 5.0) — seconds threshold for dumping benchmarks
- `spacer.gpdr` (BOOL, default false) — use GPDR strategy for non-linear CHC
- `spacer.gpdr.bfs` (BOOL, default true) — BFS exploration strategy for GPDR model search
- `spacer.use_bg_invs` (BOOL, default false) — enable external background invariants
- `spacer.use_lim_num_gen` (BOOL, default false) — enable limit-numbers generalizer
- `spacer.logic` (SYMBOL, default '') — SMT-LIB logic to configure internal SMT solvers
- `spacer.arith.solver` (UINT, default 2) — 0 none, 1 Bellman-Ford (diff logic), 2 simplex, 3 Floyd-Warshall (diff logic, no theory combo), 4 UTVPI, 5 infinitary LRA, 6 LRA solver
- `spacer.global` (BOOL, default false) — enable global guidance
- `spacer.gg.concretize` (BOOL, default true) — global guidance: concretize
- `spacer.gg.conjecture` (BOOL, default true) — global guidance: conjecture
- `spacer.gg.subsume` (BOOL, default true) — global guidance: subsume
- `spacer.use_iuc` (BOOL, default true) — enable IUC for lemma generalization
- `spacer.expand_bnd` (BOOL, default false) — enable expand-bound lemma generalization

# Part 10 — optimization, Ackermannization, parallelism, commands, API, models, parameters

## `src/opt/` — optimization and MaxSAT

### MaxSAT engine dispatch
- **Where**: `src/opt/maxsmt.cpp:183`–`:211`; param `opt.maxsat_engine` SYMBOL default `maxres` (`src/opt/opt_params.pyg`)
- **Does**: selects the MaxSAT algorithm. The table, read from the dispatch:

| `opt.maxsat_engine` | constructor | line |
|---|---|---|
| (maxlex heuristic, tried first when `opt.maxlex.enable` and the problem is lexicographic) | `mk_maxlex` | `:188` |
| `maxresw` | `mk_maxresw` | `:191` |
| `maxres` / unset / no soft constraints | `mk_maxres` | `:193` |
| `maxres-bin` | `mk_maxres_binary` | `:195` |
| `rc2` | `mk_rc2` | `:197` |
| `rc2bin` | `mk_rc2bin` | `:199` |
| `pd-maxres` | `mk_primal_dual_maxres` | `:201` |
| `wmax` | `mk_wmax` | `:203` |
| `sortmax` | `mk_sortmax` | `:205` |
| anything else | warning, falls back to `mk_maxres` | `:207` |

**`core_maxsat` is documented but not implemented.** `opt_params.pyg`'s help
string for `maxsat_engine` advertises `'core_maxsat'`, and the dispatch has no
branch for it — passing it produces `warning_msg("solver %s is not recognized,
using default 'maxres'")`. `[C]`

### MaxRes family (core-guided)
- **Where**: `src/opt/maxcore.cpp:81` (`class maxcore`); constructors `mk_maxres` `:1282`, `mk_maxresw` `:1287`, `mk_rc2` `:1291`, `mk_rc2bin` `:1297`, `mk_maxres_binary` `:1302`, `mk_primal_dual_maxres` `:1308`; trace ids `:157` (`maxres`), `:163` (`pd-maxres`), `:169` (`rc2`); LNS hook `lns_maxcore` `:101`
- **Default**: `maxres` is the default MaxSAT engine
- **Does**: relaxes unsat cores by adding at-most-one/totalizer constraints over core literals; the six constructors are weight-aware, binary-encoded, RC2, and primal-dual variants of the same loop.
- **Provenance**: `[C]`

### `maxlex`, `wmax`, `sortmax`, `totalizer`
- **Where**: `src/opt/maxlex.cpp:44`, `src/opt/wmax.cpp:34`, `src/opt/sortmax.cpp:31`, `src/opt/totalizer.cpp`
- **Default**: `maxlex` is tried first when `opt.maxlex.enable` (True) and the objective is lexicographic; the others need `opt.maxsat_engine`
- **Does**: lexicographic MaxSAT shortcut; weighted-theory-solver MaxSAT; sorting-network encoding of the cardinality objective; the totalizer encoding used by `rc2` (`opt.rc2.totalizer`, True).
- **Provenance**: `[C]`

### `optsmt` — the arithmetic optimization loop
- **Where**: `src/opt/optsmt.cpp:811` (`symba` branch), `:832`; param `opt.optsmt_engine` SYMBOL default `basic`, alternative `symba`
- **Does**: `basic` improves an objective by re-solving with tightened bounds; `symba` is the Symba unbounded-objective algorithm.
- **Provenance**: `[C]`

### `opt_nlsat` — exact optimization over NLSAT cells
- **Where**: `src/opt/opt_nlsat.cpp:59` (`nra_fragment_check`), `:94` (`uninterp_const_collector`); params `opt.optsmt_nlsat` BOOL **True**, `opt.pareto_nlsat` BOOL **True**, `opt.optsmt_bisect_rounds` UINT 64
- **Does**: when the problem is in the NRA fragment, optimizes real objectives exactly over nlsat cells (algebraic optima print as `root-obj`) and proves unboundedness with nlqsat queries, rather than bisecting a float interval.
- **Provenance**: `[C]`

### `opt_pareto`, `opt_lns`, `opt_cores`, `opt_preprocess`, `pb_sls`
- **Where**: `src/opt/opt_pareto.cpp`, `src/opt/opt_lns.cpp:150` (`scoped_bounding`), `src/opt/opt_cores.cpp:194`, `src/opt/opt_preprocess.cpp:101`/`:186`, `src/opt/pb_sls.cpp:66`
- **Default**: Pareto via `opt.priority=pareto`; LNS behind `opt.enable_lns` (False); SLS tuning behind `opt.enable_sls` (False); core rotation behind `opt.enable_core_rotate` (False)
- **Does**: Pareto-front enumeration; large-neighbourhood search over weighted MaxSAT assignments; core sampling; soft-constraint preprocessing (mutexes, dominance); a pseudo-Boolean local search.
- **Provenance**: `[C]`

### `opt` parameters (complete)
- **Where**: `src/opt/opt_params.pyg`
- **Does**: `optsmt_engine` (`basic`), `optsmt_bisect_rounds` (64), `optsmt_nlsat` (True), `pareto_nlsat` (True), `maxsat_engine` (`maxres`), `priority` (`lex`), `dump_benchmarks` (False), `dump_models` (False), `solution_prefix` (''), `timeout` (UINT_MAX), `rlimit` (0), `enable_sls` (False), `enable_lns` (False), `lns_conflicts` (1000), `enable_core_rotate` (False), `enable_sat` (True), `elim_01` (True), `incremental` (False), `pp.neat` (True), `pb.compile_equality` (False), `pp.wcnf` (False), `maxlex.enable` (True), `rc2.totalizer` (True), `maxres.hill_climb` (True), `maxres.add_upper_bound_block` (False), `maxres.max_num_cores` (200), `maxres.max_core_size` (3), `maxres.maximize_assignment` (False), `maxres.max_correction_set_size` (3), `maxres.wmax` (False), `maxres.pivot_on_correction_set` (True).

## `src/ackermannization/` — eager and lazy Ackermannization

### `ackermannize_bv_tactic` (eager)
- **Where**: `src/ackermannization/ackermannize_bv_tactic.cpp`; params `src/ackermannization/ackermannization_params.pyg:5` (`eager`, BOOL, **True**) and `ackermannize_bv_tactic_params.pyg` (`div0_ackermann_limit`, UINT, 1000)
- **Logics**: reached from the QF_UFBV / QF_AUFBV tactic pipelines
- **Does**: replaces uninterpreted function applications over bit-vectors with fresh constants plus congruence lemmas, so the goal becomes pure QF_BV and can be bit-blasted.
- **Provenance**: `[C]`

### `lackr` (lazy Ackermannization with CEGAR)
- **Where**: `src/ackermannization/lackr.cpp`, `lackr_model_constructor.cpp`, `lackr_model_converter_lazy.cpp`
- **Default**: `ackermannization.eager=false`
- **Does**: solves the Ackermann-abstracted problem, checks the model against the real congruences, and adds only the violated congruence lemmas — a counterexample-guided abstraction loop over UF.
- **Provenance**: `[C]`

### `ackr_bound_probe`
- **Where**: `src/ackermannization/ackr_bound_probe.cpp`
- **Does**: estimates the number of congruence lemmas eager Ackermannization would produce, so a tactic can choose between eager and lazy.
- **Provenance**: `[C]`

## Parallel solving

### `parallel::worker` — parallel SMT with cube sharing
- **Where**: `src/smt/smt_parallel.cpp:67` (namespace under `#else` of `SINGLE_THREAD`), main loop `:1885`, `worker::run` `:745`, `share_units` `:976`, `worker::simplify` `:1019`, `find_backbone_candidates` `:909`, `batch_manager::check` `:1104`, `backtrack` `:1182`
- **Default**: `smt.threads` UINT **1** (`smt_params_helper.pyg:37`), i.e. off; `smt.threads.max_conflicts` 400, `smt.threads.cube_frequency` 2
- **Does**: splits the problem into cubes, farms them to worker contexts, shares learned units, and backtracks the cube tree on unsat cores.
- **Provenance**: `[C]`

### `backbones_worker` — Janota-style backbone computation
- **Where**: `src/smt/smt_parallel.cpp:109` (`run`), `:116` (`run_failed_literal_mode`), `:228` (`probe_literal`), `:261` (`run_batch_mode`), `:953` (`try_get_unit_backbone`), `:526` (`collect_global_backbone`)
- **Default**: `parallel.num_bb_threads` UINT **2** (`src/solver/parallel_params.pyg`) — 0 off, 1 negative mode only, 2 negative and positive
- **Does**: dedicated threads that compute backbone literals (literals true in every model) by failed-literal probing and feed them back as units.
- **Provenance**: `[C]`

### `core_minimizer_worker`
- **Where**: `src/smt/smt_parallel.cpp:620`, `minimize_unsat_core` `:646`, `run` `:723`; `batch_manager::enqueue_core_minimization` `:1194`
- **Default**: `parallel.core_minimize` BOOL **True**
- **Does**: a thread pool that minimizes the unsat cores used for cube backtracking, so a refuted cube prunes more of the tree.
- **Provenance**: `[C]`

### `sls_worker`
- **Where**: `src/smt/smt_parallel.cpp:73` (`run`), `:884`; allocates `sls::smt_solver` at `:888`
- **Default**: did not verify the enabling condition
- **Does**: runs the local-search engine as one of the parallel workers.
- **Provenance**: `[C]`

### `parallel_tactical` / `mk_parallel_tactic`
- **Where**: `src/solver/parallel_tactical.h:25`; callers `src/sat/sat_solver/inc_sat_solver.cpp:1314`, `src/tactic/fd_solver/fd_solver.cpp:40`, `src/smt/tactic/smt_tactic_core.cpp:433`/`:439`
- **Default**: `parallel.enable` BOOL **False**
- **Does**: a cube-and-conquer tactic wrapper around any solver, with its own simplify/conquer schedule.
- **Provenance**: `[C]`

### `parallel` parameters (complete)
- **Where**: `src/solver/parallel_params.pyg`
- **Does**: `enable` (False), `threads.max` (10000), `num_bb_threads` (2), `core_minimize` (True), `ablate_backtracking` (False), `cube.lookahead` (False — otherwise VSIDS activity picks the split literal), `conquer.batch_size` (100), `conquer.restart.max` (5), `conquer.delay` (10), `conquer.backtrack_frequency` (10), `simplify.exp` (1), `simplify.max_conflicts` (UINT_MAX), `simplify.restart.max` (5000), `simplify.inprocess.max` (2).
- **Provenance**: `[C]`

## Non-standard SMT-LIB commands (each names a technique)

Installed from `src/shell/smtlib_frontend.cpp:164`–`:170`
(`install_dl_cmds`, `install_dbg_cmds`, `install_polynomial_cmds`,
`install_subpaving_cmds`, `install_opt_cmds`, `install_proof_cmds`).

| command | where | does |
|---|---|---|
| `(check-sat-using <tactic>)` | `src/cmd_context/tactic_cmds.cpp` | run an explicit tactic instead of the default strategy |
| `(apply <tactic>)` | `src/cmd_context/tactic_cmds.cpp` | apply a tactic and print the resulting goals |
| `(declare-tactic <name> <t>)` | `src/cmd_context/tactic_cmds.cpp` | name a tactic expression |
| `(set-simplifier <s>)` | `src/cmd_context/simplifier_cmds.cpp` | install an explicit simplifier pipeline (39 registered simplifiers) |
| `(simplify <t>)` | `src/cmd_context/simplify_cmd.cpp` | run the rewriter on a term |
| `(eval <t>)` | `src/cmd_context/eval_cmd.cpp` | evaluate a term in the current model |
| `(get-consequences ...)` | `src/cmd_context/cmd_context.cpp` | consequence finding / implied literals |
| `(get-interpolant ...)` | `src/cmd_context/cmd_context.cpp` | Craig interpolation |
| `(mbp ...)`, `(mbp-qel ...)` | `src/qe/qe_cmd.cpp` | model-based projection |
| `(qe-lite ...)`, `(qel ...)` | `src/qe/qe_cmd.cpp` | light quantifier elimination |
| `(eufi ...)`, `(euf-project ...)` | `src/cmd_context/extra_cmds/dbg_cmds.cpp:483`, `:535` | EUF interpolation / EUF projection |
| `(to-poly ...)` | `src/cmd_context/extra_cmds/polynomial_cmds.cpp:200` | convert to polynomial normal form |
| `(to-subpaving ...)`, `(mul_to_power ...)` | `src/cmd_context/extra_cmds/subpaving_cmds.cpp:50`, `:34` | hand a goal to the interval-subpaving engine |
| `(infer ...)`, `(assume ...)`, `(del ...)`, `(deps ...)` | `src/cmd_context/extra_cmds/proof_cmds.cpp:409`, `:379`, `:268`, `:177` | replay and check a clausal (RUP/DRAT) proof |
| `(display-dimacs)` | `src/cmd_context/extra_cmds/dbg_cmds.cpp:337` | dump the internalized CNF |
| `(set-initial-value ...)` | `src/cmd_context/cmd_context.cpp` | seed the solver's phase/value for a variable |
| `(declare-map ...)` | `src/cmd_context/cmd_context.cpp` | lift a function pointwise over arrays |
| `(prefer ...)`, `(reset-preferences)` | `src/cmd_context/cmd_context.cpp` | did not verify |
| ~24 `dbg-*` commands | `src/cmd_context/extra_cmds/dbg_cmds.cpp` | direct access to individual rewriters (`dbg-bool-rewriter`, `dbg-th-rewriter` `:775`, `dbg-elim-unused-vars` `:261`, `dbg-instantiate` `:318`, `dbg-instantiate-nested` `:323`, …) |

**Provenance**: `[C]` for every path:line; the "does" column is `[C]` where the
command name is self-describing and `[I]` where I read only the registration.

procedure behind them.

## Dead, forced-off, or unreachable — my area

### `xor_solver` (EUF core) — excluded from the build AND hardcoded off
- `src/sat/smt/xor_solver.cpp` is absent from `src/sat/smt/CMakeLists.txt`; a stale build artefact `xor_solver.d` sits beside it; `src/sat/sat_config.cpp:251` sets `m_xor_solver = false; // prevent users from playing with this option`. `[C]`

### `sat.bv_solver=1` (polysat) — a parameter value whose implementation is a throw
- `src/sat/smt/euf_solver.cpp:143`–`:144`: `throw default_exception("polysat solver is not integrated")`. Documented as a legal value at `src/params/smt_params_helper.pyg:63`. `[C]`

### `opt.maxsat_engine=core_maxsat` — documented, not implemented
- Advertised in the `opt_params.pyg` help string; no branch in `src/opt/maxsmt.cpp:183`–`:211`; falls through to `warning_msg` + `maxres`. `[C]`

### `euf-completion` — registered simplifier, never in the default pipeline
- `src/solver/solver_preprocess.cpp:83`–`:86` carries `// add: euf_completion?` as a live TODO. `[C]`

### No sequence/string theory in the EUF core
- `src/sat/smt/euf_solver.cpp:138`–`:161` has no seq branch. `sat.euf=true` on a QF_S goal reaches `unhandled_function` (`:180`). `[C]`

### `if (false && …)` and `#if 0` census
- 34 permanently-false conditionals and 154 files containing an `#if 0` block, across the whole tree (`grep -rn "if (false\|if(false\|&& false)" src/ --include=*.cpp --include=*.h | wc -l` → 34; `grep -rl "^#if 0" src/ --include=*.cpp --include=*.h | wc -l` → 154).
- In my area specifically: `src/sat/smt/euf_proof_checker.cpp:503` (`if (false && is_rup(proof_hint))`), `src/sat/sat_solver.cpp:4376`, `src/sat/smt/intblast_solver.cpp:185` (`if (false)`), `src/sat/smt/arith_solver.cpp:1538` (`if (ineq.cmp() == lp::EQ && false)`), `src/sat/smt/bv_delay_internalize.cpp:235` (`if (bv.is_zero(arg_value) && false)`), `src/opt/opt_preprocess.cpp:237` (`if (false && !prop_mutexes(soft, lower))`).
- One carries its own history: `src/smt/theory_arith_nl.h:1138` — `else if (false && m_util.is_add(m)) // introduced by #4532, disabled for #4765`. A technique that shipped, caused a bug, and was disabled in place rather than removed. `[C]`

---

# Dead, forced-off, and unreachable — consolidated index

Each area's own `Dead or unreachable` section carries the evidence; this is the
index. A technique here is *implemented in the tree* and *not reachable by a
default configuration* — the most valuable rows in a capability diff, because
they are things the shipping solver does not actually do.

## Never built

| what | where | evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `xor_solver` (XOR / Gaussian elimination in the EUF core) | `src/sat/smt/xor_solver.cpp` | absent from `src/sat/smt/CMakeLists.txt`; stale `xor_solver.d` beside it; `sat_config.cpp:251` hardcodes `m_xor_solver = false` |
| `smt_induction` (induction lemma generation) | `src/smt/smt_induction.cpp.disabled`, `.h.disabled` | renamed out of the build; sole call site `smt_context.cpp:1761` is inside `#if 0` (`:1760`–`:1764`); `smt.induction` param and `smt_params.h:116` field both still live |
| `dl_mk_different` (Horn rule transform) | `src/muz/transforms/dl_mk_different.h` | header only, no `.cpp`, zero occurrences in `src/muz/transforms/CMakeLists.txt` |

## Built, never constructed

| what | where | evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `sat::bcd` — blocked-clause decomposition (Biere & Heule, LPAR-19) | `src/sat/sat_bcd.cpp` | only tree reference is `friend class bcd;` at `sat_solver.h:218` |
| `mk_synchronize` — Horn rule merging (~300 lines) | `src/muz/transforms/dl_mk_synchronize.cpp:28` | zero `alloc()` sites |
| `theory_si_arith`, `theory_smi_arith` | `src/smt/theory_arith.h:1276`–`:1277` | the typedefs themselves are commented out; the types do not exist in compiled code |
| `install_qe_cmd` — the `(elim-quantifiers)` command | `src/qe/qe_cmd.cpp` | zero callers |

## Reachable only from a test

| what | where |
|---|---|
| `npn3_finder` — NPN-equivalence gate detection (mux/maj/or-and/xor) | `src/sat/sat_npn3_finder.cpp`; sole user `src/test/finder.cpp`; the production ANF pass uses `xor_finder`/`aig_finder` and never includes it |

## A parameter value with no implementation

| parameter | documented as | what actually happens |
|---|---|---|
| `smt.bv.solver=1` | "polysat" (`smt_params_helper.pyg:63`) | `throw default_exception("polysat solver is not integrated")` (`euf_solver.cpp:143`) |
| `opt.maxsat_engine=core_maxsat` | listed in the help string (`opt_params.pyg`) | no dispatch branch; `warning_msg(... "using default 'maxres'")` (`maxsmt.cpp:207`) |
| `sat.local_search_mode=qsat` | "either default wsat or qsat" (`sat_params.pyg`) | enum is `{gsat, wsat}` (`sat_config.h:82`); anything but `gsat` silently becomes `wsat` (`sat_config.cpp:104`) |
| `smt.induction=true` | "enable generation of induction lemmas" | nothing — see *Never built* |

## Registered but not in any default pipeline

- **`euf-completion`** — congruence-closure-modulo simplification; a live
  `// add: euf_completion?` TODO sits at `src/solver/solver_preprocess.cpp:85`.
- **`ufbv-rewriter`** — registered with the wrong body: its `ADD_TACTIC` string
  at `src/tactic/ufbv/ufbv_rewriter_tactic.h:28` reads
  `"mk_quasi_macros_tactic(m, p)"`, so `(apply ufbv-rewriter)` runs quasi-macros.
- **`card2bv`** — never reaches a plain `(check-sat)` on the main SMT path; only
  the finite-domain simplifier (`src/tactic/fd_solver/fd_simplifier.cpp:322`)
  runs it.
- **`smtfd`, `qfufbv_ackr`, `fm`, `qe`, `dom-simplify`, `bvarray2uf`,
  `split-clause`** and roughly three dozen further registered tactics — compiled
  and scriptable, wired into no default pipeline.
- **Classical `qe.cpp`** (arith / bool / BV / array / datatype / DL plugin
  family, ~2700 lines) — superseded in every default strategy by `qe-light` +
  `qsat`; kept alive by the standalone `qe` tactic and tests.
- **`qe_mbi`** (`(get-interpolant)`) — reachable only through that one command.
- **`tab`, `clp`, `ddnf`, `qbmc` fixedpoint engines** — live code, selectable
  only by an `fp.engine` value the parameter's own help text omits.
- **`mk_mip_tactic`** — referenced from commented-out lines in `qflia_tactic.cpp`
  and a `#if 0` block in `qflra_tactic.cpp`; **the function does not exist**
  anywhere in the tree. Its companion `mk_is_mip_probe` is defined, called only
  from that dead block, and never `ADD_PROBE`-registered.
- **`mk_qfufnra_tactic`** — does not exist; `default_tactic.cpp:51`'s dispatch
  for it is commented out, while the `is-qfufnra` probe is live and drives
  nothing.

## Reachable, but only off the default path

These are *not* dead and should not be counted as absent capability.

- **`theory_arith` and everything under it** — Simplex, Gomory cuts, the old
  ast-based Gröbner module, difference logic (`theory_dense_*`, `theory_idl`,
  `theory_rdl`, `theory_fidl`, `theory_frdl`), UTVPI — registered at a dozen
  sites in `smt_setup.cpp` under `m_arith_mode == AS_OLD_ARITH`, i.e.
  `smt.arith.solver` values other than the default 6. See *Corrections*.
- **`distribute_forall` (the `src/ast/rewriter/` copy)** — its only includer is
  `src/solver/assertions/asserted_formulas.h`, which is live: `asserted_formulas
  m_asserted_formulas` is a member of `smt_context` (`src/smt/smt_context.h:112`).
  So the legacy pipeline is reachable, and this file is not dead.
- **`src/math/subpaving/`** — reachable through `(to-subpaving)`, installed by
  `src/shell/smtlib_frontend.cpp:167`; not part of any solving pipeline.
- **`src/math/realclosure/`** — API-only (`Z3_rcf_*`); nlsat uses
  `polynomial::algebraic_numbers`, a different implementation
  (`nlsat_types.h:194`).
- **`src/math/hilbert/`** — consumed only by the Datalog/CHC Karr-invariant
  code (`src/muz/rel/karr_relation.h`, `src/muz/transforms/dl_mk_karr_invariants.h`).
- **Relevancy-based case splits** (`CS_RELEVANCY`, `CS_RELEVANCY_ACTIVITY`,
  `CS_RELEVANCY_GOAL`) — silently downgraded to `CS_ACTIVITY` under auto-config
  or low relevancy; easy to request, hard to actually get.
- **`PS_THEORY` phase selection** — its only candidate call site is commented
  out; dead in every shipped logic configuration.

## Permanently-false conditionals

Tree-wide census at commit `e18d63b`: **34** occurrences of `if (false …)` /
`… && false)` and **154** files containing an `#if 0` block
(`grep -rn "if (false\|if(false\|&& false)" src/ --include=*.cpp --include=*.h | wc -l`;
`grep -rl "^#if 0" src/ --include=*.cpp --include=*.h | wc -l`).

One is worth reading for its comment alone —
`src/smt/theory_arith_nl.h:1138`:

```cpp
else if (false && m_util.is_add(m)) // introduced by #4532, disabled for #4765
```

A technique that shipped, caused a regression, and was disabled in place rather
than removed. Each area section lists the occurrences in its own files.
